This is a list of chess openings, organised by the Encyclopaedia of Chess Openings (ECO) code classification system. The chess openings are categorised into five broad areas ("A" through "E"), with each of those broken up into one hundred subcategories ("00" through "99"). The openings were published in five volumes of ECO, with volumes labeled "A" through "E".[1][2]

This article uses algebraic notation to describe chess moves.

A – Flank openings

[edit]

White first moves other than 1.e4, 1.d4 (A00–A39)

  • White first moves other than 1.c4 (A00–A09): Atypical openings
  • 1.c4: English Opening (A10–A39)

1.d4 without 1...d5 or 1...Nf6: Atypical Semi-Closed Games (A40–A99)

  • 1.d4 without 1...d5, 1...Nf6 or 1...f5: Atypical replies to 1.d4 (A40–A44)
  • 1.d4 Nf6 without 2.c4: Atypical replies to 1...Nf6 (A45–A49)
  • 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 without 2...e6 or 2...g6: Atypical Indian systems (A50–A79)
  • 1.d4 f5: Dutch Defence (A80–A99)

A00–A09

[edit]

White first moves other than 1.e4, 1.d4, 1.c4:

D – Closed Games and Semi-Closed Games

  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Positional Defense: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 d6 3.Nc3 Nbd7 4.e4 e5 5.Nf3 g6 6.Be2 Bg7 7.0-0 0-0
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Ukrainian Defense: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 a5
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Donner Defense: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 c6
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Glek Defense: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Na6
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Positional Defense, Closed Line: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 Nbd7 7.0-0 e5 8.d5
  • E95 King's Indian, Orthodox, 7...Nbd7, 8.Re1
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nbd7 8.Re1
  • E96 King's Indian, Orthodox, 7...Nbd7, Main line
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Positional Defense, Main Line: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nbd7 8.Re1 c6 9.Bf1 a5
  • E97 King's Indian, Orthodox, Aronin–Taimanov Variation (Yugoslav Attack / Mar del Plata Variation)
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Aronin-Taimanov Defense: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nc6
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Bayonet Attack: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nc6 8.d5 Ne7 9.b4
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Bayonet Attack, Yepishin's Line: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nc6 8.d5 Ne7 9.b4 Nh5 10.Qc2
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Bayonet Attack, Sokolov's Line: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nc6 8.d5 Ne7 9.b4 Nh5 10.Re1
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Korchnoi Attack: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nc6 8.d5 Ne7 9.Bd2
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Modern System: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nc6 8.d5 Ne7 9.Nd2
  • E98 King's Indian, Orthodox, Aronin–Taimanov, 9.Ne1
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Classical System: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nc6 8.d5 Ne7 9.Ne1
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Classical System, Neo-Classical Line: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nc6 8.d5 Ne7 9.Ne1 Nd7 10.Be3
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Classical System, Kozul Gambit: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nc6 8.d5 Ne7 9.Ne1 Nd7 10.Be3 f5 11.f3 f4 12.Bf2 g5 13.Rc1 Ng6 14.c5
  • E99 King's Indian, Orthodox, Aronin–Taimanov, Defence
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Classical System, Traditional Line: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nc6 8.d5 Ne7 9.Ne1 Nd7 10.f3 f5
  • King's Indian Defense: Orthodox Variation, Classical System, Benko Attack: 1.d4 Nf6 2.c4 g6 3.Nc3 Bg7 4.e4 d6 5.Nf3 0-0 6.Be2 e5 7.0-0 Nc6 8.d5 Ne7 9.Ne1 Nd7 10.f3 f5 11.g4

Statistics

[edit]
Moves 1800–1900 1901–1935 1935–1998
1.e4 e5 63% 31% 15%
1.e4 other 23% 20% 35%
1.d4 d5 10% 28% 15%
1.d4 other 3% 16% 23%
1.other 5% 12%

These statistics show a movement away from symmetrical defences to asymmetrical defences. In particular, in reply to 1.e4, the Sicilian and French defences, and to 1.d4 the Indian defences. Also, particularly amongst strong players, an increased use of the English Opening for White.[3]
